The hooks (`useVendorResearch.ts`) read, save and remove the key through `vendorResearch.repository.ts`; a save or a removal invalidates the search key and the search status queries.
- States: loading, empty, a failed load with **Retry** (`InlineError`), and not set up. Research is not set up only when the organization has no LLM key (`hasLlmKey` is false): the section says "Vendor research is not set up." to everyone, and only Admins also see the hint "Add an LLM key under Settings → API Keys, in the LLM Keys section.". The outcome `search_failed` is labelled "could not be researched".

**Row button.** `components/VendorResearchButton/` is a microscope icon button (tooltip "Research risks") that `components/Table/WithPlaceholder` renders in the actions of each vendor row. It is hidden without `vendor.edit`, and disabled while the settings load and when research is not set up (no LLM key). If the request for the settings fails, the button and the findings section treat research as set up and the server decides: a start without an LLM key is refused with a 409. The global error toast is off for the start call, so every click is answered with a message through `onNotify` (started, already running, cooldown, daily cap, not set up, queue unavailable, or a generic failure).

**Data layer.**

| File (under `Clients/src/`) | Purpose |
|-----------------------------|---------|
| `application/repository/vendorResearch.repository.ts` | The eight calls (the six of the tab and the two of the settings; `listVendorRiskFindings` takes an optional `{ suppressGlobalErrorToast }`, which the count of the tab sets). A failure is an `APIError` that keeps the HTTP status; `readVendorResearchError` reads `code` and `retryAfterSeconds` from the response body |
| `application/hooks/useVendorResearch.ts` | React Query hooks under the query key `["vendorResearch"]` (`useVendorResearchSettings` reads the choice, `useSaveVendorResearchSettings` saves it). Accepting invalidates the vendor risks query, the research queries and `VENDOR_INSIGHTS_KEY`; dismissing invalidates the research queries. The follow hint has its own key, `["vendorResearchFollow"]` |
| `domain/interfaces/i.vendorResearch.ts` | Types that mirror `Servers/domain.layer/interfaces/i.vendorResearch.ts` (snake_case, dates as ISO strings), and `AcceptFindingBody`, the owner and edits of an accept |
| `application/utils/vendorResearchText.ts` | Labels, and translated sentences for the closed set of phrases the server stores. A stored string it does not know is never shown; the model's own category note is shown as plain text under "AI note (unverified)". Also the credibility (`credibilityLabel`, `sourceCategoryLabel`, `recordStatusLabel`, `credibilityRationale`, `nameOnlyNote`) and the coverage of a run (`coverageLines`) |
| `application/utils/vendorResearchList.ts` | The order of the rows, their groups and the counts of the summary (`orderFindings`, `groupFindings`, `summarizeFindings`) |
| `application/utils/vendorResearchPlainText.ts` | The plain words of the section: the sentences of the summary (`pendingSentence`, `topicsSentence`, `uncheckedSentence`, `earlierLegend`), the line and the cautions of a row (`credibilityLine`, `rowCautions`), the tags of a source (`sourceTag`, `recordTag`), the line of the scales (`scalesLine`) and the link of a source (`readSourceLink`) |
| `application/utils/vendorResearchAlerts.ts` | The messages for a start, shared by the findings section and the row button |
| `application/utils/vendorResearchPreview.ts` | `defaultActionOwner` (the owner a preview starts on) and `toAcceptBody` (the accept request for what the vendor risk form saved) |

Strings are translated by the DOM translator from `i18n/translations.ts` (de, fr and es). The stored phrases of a run are English and the client maps them. The only server messages the research feature adds are the seven 400 sentences of the accept edits ("The risk description must be 1 to 500 characters", "Invalid likelihood", ...), which are in `Servers/locales/{en,de,fr}.json`; the form checks the same limits before it saves, so a person meets them only through a client that bypasses it.

## Automation Triggers

| Trigger | Event |
|---------|-------|
| `vendor_added` | New vendor created |
| `vendor_updated` | Vendor modified |
| `vendor_deleted` | Vendor deleted |
| `vendor_review_date_approaching` | Daily sweep: the review date is today plus the automation's `daysBefore` (UTC days) |

`vendor_added`, `vendor_updated` and `vendor_deleted` run every action of every active automation of the trigger; an automation that existed before that change keeps only the event's first action until it is edited and saved (`legacy_first_action_only`). See [Automations → Dispatch of entity events](../infrastructure/automations.md#dispatch-of-entity-events).

Review reminders exist only as this automation: an organization without an
active `vendor_review_date_approaching` automation gets no reminder email or
in-app notice. The Home queue lists due vendors regardless. Both use the same
rule for `Reviewed` vendors (dropped only once the review date has passed). The
sweep runs every action of every matching automation and sends one in-app
notice per vendor per run. The vendor form stores the picked day at noon UTC
(`calendarDayAtNoonUtc`), so its UTC day is the day the user picked; a vendor
without a review date gets no reminder; vendors
saved before that may read a day early for users east of UTC until next saved.
The job is scheduled at midnight UTC (`tz: "UTC"`). The in-app notice's
title and message and the email subject are i18n keys
(`notifyVendorReviewDue`, `services/inAppNotification.service.ts`), so they
reach the recipient in their language; the email body stays English. See
[Automations → Vendor Review Notifications](../infrastructure/automations.md#vendor-review-notifications).

## Validation Rules

### Required Fields

- vendor_name
- vendor_provides
- assignee (valid user ID)
- website
- vendor_contact_person

### Demo Vendors

Vendors with `is_demo=true` are sample data, written by the demo seed
(`infrastructure.layer/driver/autoDriver.driver.ts`) through the query layer.
A create through the service that sends `is_demo: true` is refused
(`canBeModified`, 403 "Demo vendors cannot be modified"), so the API can't
make new demo vendors. Existing demo vendors and their risks can be edited
and deleted like any other: that is deliberate, so an organization can work
with the sample data it starts with.

## Key Files

### Backend

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `domain.layer/models/vendor/vendor.model.ts` | Vendor model |
| `domain.layer/models/vendorRisk/vendorRisk.model.ts` | Risk model |
| `domain.layer/models/vendorsProjects/vendorsProjects.model.ts` | Junction model |
| `utils/vendor.utils.ts` | Vendor queries |
| `utils/vendorRisk.utils.ts` | Risk queries |
| `services/vendor.service.ts` | Vendor create, update, delete (REST and Advisor), including the other names and identifiers |
| `services/vendorRisk.service.ts` | Vendor risk create, update, delete (REST and Advisor) |
| `controllers/vendor.ctrl.ts` | Controller; `createVendor` starts the automatic research after the service has saved the vendor (console creates only, not Advisor ones). `createVendorV1` and `updateVendorByIdV1` run the same create and update with the other names and identifiers; the legacy `createVendor` and `updateVendorById` drop those fields (`withoutV1OnlyVendorFields`) |
| `routes/vendor.route.ts` | Routes (vendor CRUD, legacy `/api/vendors`) |
| `services/riskLinks/vendorReports.ts` | Exposure, duplicate and coverage reports |
| `utils/vendorRiskReport.utils.ts` | Queries behind those reports |
| `services/riskLinks/vendorCandidates.ts` | Vendor risk candidate notices |
| `services/riskLinks/vendorRelated.ts` | Related vendor risk scoring and recompute |
| `utils/vendorRiskLink.utils.ts` | Queries behind related vendor risk pairs |
| `controllers/vendorResearch.ctrl.ts` | Research endpoints: start, latest run, config, findings list, accept, dismiss, the trace of a run, the decision statistics and the settings |
| `routes/v1/vendors.route.ts` | `POST /v1/vendors` and `PATCH /v1/vendors/:id` (create and update with the other names and identifiers), and `/v1/vendors/:id/research`: start a run, the latest run and the trace of a run |
| `routes/v1/vendorResearch.route.ts` | `/v1/vendor-research/config`, `/v1/vendor-research/decision-stats`, `/v1/vendor-research/search-status`, `/v1/vendor-research/search-key` (GET, PUT, DELETE) and `/v1/vendor-research/settings` |
| `routes/v1/vendorRiskFindings.route.ts` | `/v1/vendor-risk-findings` list, accept and dismiss |
| `services/vendorResearch/trigger.ts` | `startVendorResearch` (checks, queued run, job) and `triggerAutoResearch` |
| `services/vendorResearch/job.ts` | `handleVendorResearchJob`: runs the pipeline, then the assignee notice |
| `services/vendorResearch/pipeline.ts` | `runVendorResearch`: one run from `queued` to `completed` or `failed` |
| `services/vendorResearch/categories.ts`, `target.ts`, `evidence.ts` | The lookback windows and the categories a vendor allows, the vendor's name and domain, the evidence pack built from the ledger and the provenance of its sources (`provenanceOf`: the database of a record, the canonical address a copy names) |
| `services/vendorResearch/researchAgent.ts` | `createVendorResearcher`: one web session, page reader and research loop per run, with the vendor's prompts |
| `services/vendorResearch/preflight.ts` | The check of the vendor's website before the loop: `checkWebsite`, the briefing text, the closed hosts and the calls recorded for a closed site |
| `services/vendorResearch/gather.ts` | The routine work that code does before the model's first step: the news searches, the pages of the vendor's site, and the digest of what was collected for the briefing |
| `services/vendorResearch/records/` | The lookups a run still makes itself (`createRecordsLookup`): Have I Been Pwned (`hibp.ts`) and the incident history on the status page of the vendor (`statuspage.ts`, fetched by `feedFetch.ts` with a `robots.txt` check), with the exact match of a vendor (`match.ts`), the memory of the answers (`memo.ts`), the limiters of the NVD, the SEC and GLEIF (`spacer.ts`, `sharedSpacer.ts`) and the rest after a failure; `sharedRecordsLookup` is the one of the process. `sec.ts`, `ofac.ts`, `kev.ts`, `nvd.ts` and `sentence.ts` keep what the vendor risk database still imports from the former per-run lookups (names, addresses, the stored CIK, the words of a record) |
| `services/vendorResearch/edgarFiler.ts` | `createEdgarFilerResolver`: who filed an EDGAR filing, read from the filing's SGML header (`accessionOfFiling`, `secHeaderUrl`, `parseFilingRoles`, `filerRoleOf`), for the Q28 rule of the registry |
| `services/vendorResearch/searchEngineOf.ts` | `searchEngineOf`: the search engine of a key (Claude's search or GDELT), told from the name and the address of the research key; `searchEngineWith` and `searchSpecOf`: the engine once the organization's Brave key is counted (`brave` for a key with no search of its own), with the Brave key on the spec as a property that is not enumerable; `withoutBrave` for the live evaluation |
| `services/vendorResearch/braveKeyCheck.ts` | `checkBraveKey`: the check of a Brave Search key before it is stored (see [Brave text is never stored](#brave-text-is-never-stored)) |
| `services/vendorResearch/hints.ts` | The cheap model call that turns what the model remembers about a vendor into search queries (never evidence) |
| `services/vendorResearch/loops.ts` | How the model's budget is split between the loops of a run that has one for each category (`loopGrants`), and how their endings become the ending of the run (`combineEndings`) |
| `services/vendorResearch/researchCache.ts` | `sharedWebCache`: the application's Redis behind the cache that runs share (`robots.txt`, pages, searches), with its lookups counted |
| `services/vendorResearch/pageRenderer.ts` | `sharedPageRenderer`: the one headless browser of the process (none unless `VENDOR_RESEARCH_HEADLESS` is `true`), built on first use and handed to the page reader of every run |
| `services/vendorResearch/sourceRegistry/publisher.ts` | `publisherKey`: the publisher of a host, so that `bbc.co.uk` and `bbc.com` are one |
| `services/vendorResearch/summary.ts` | The outcome and the fixed note of each category, made from the calls of the loop |
| `services/vendorResearch/keySelection.ts`, `settings.service.ts` | Which key the research and the analysis use (`selectKeys`), and the read and the write of the choice with the check that a key is the organization's |
| `services/vendorResearch/prompts.ts`, `schema.ts` | The prompts of the loop, the analysis and the verifier (`CREDIBILITY_PROMPT_VERSION`, now `vr-20`; the analysis asks for no confidence) and the output schema of the analysis |
| `services/vendorResearch/validation.ts`, `dedupe.ts`, `text.ts` | Validation of the answer, de-duplication, text cleaning |
| `services/vendorResearch/clusters.ts` | The events of the evidence pack: the sources that report one event (`clusterSources`), shown together to the analysis and counted as one finding |
| `services/vendorResearch/analysis.ts` | `analyse`: the model call over the evidence pack, validation, de-duplication, the caps, the check of the findings that rest on snippets and the verifier; the pipeline and the evaluation both call it |
| `services/vendorResearch/verifySnippets.ts` | `verifySnippetFindings`: the page of the best source of a finding that cites search results only is read after the analysis, and a quote it does not hold marks the finding `snippet_only`; `checkSnippetFindings` gives each finding with the verdict of its check, which the credibility label reads |
| `services/vendorResearch/sourceRegistry/entries.ts`, `classify.ts` | The [source registry](#credibility) (`REGISTRY_VERSION` sr-6, 119 entries, no pattern entry) and the classifier of a cited address (`classifySource`), of a record (`classifyRecord`, `recordMatchOf`) and of the pages where the vendor states what binds it (`isStatementPage`) |
| `services/vendorResearch/credibility.ts` | `credibilityOf`: the credibility label of a finding and its basis, from its sources, their provenance and the snippet check |
| `services/vendorResearch/credibilityText.ts` | `credibilityRationale`: the English line of a label, from its basis; the API adds it to every finding |
| `services/vendorResearch/vrdb/` | The [vendor risk database](#vendor-risk-database): the lists (`sources.ts`), the bundles and their load (`bundle.ts`, `loadBundled.ts`), its queue and worker (`queue.ts`, `worker.ts`), the match keys (`keys.ts`), the run-time lookup (`lookup.ts`), the read-through (`readThrough.ts`), the gates (`gates.ts`: `SEC_CONTACT`, `UN_PERMISSION_GRANTED`, `CVE_TERMS_APPROVED`, `AIID_PERMISSION_GRANTED`), the status job (`relabel.ts`), the XML scanner (`xmlScan.ts`), the zip reader of the CVE deltas, of the Excel export of the AI Incident Database and, as a stream, of the NHTSA flat files (`zip.ts`, with the reader of the one sheet it needs in `xlsx.ts`) and a connector for each list (`connectors/`, with the kit of the lists added on 2026-11-10 in `connectors/kit/`: the factory of a file list, the person rules in `persons.ts` and the pause between requests in `pause.ts`; the pager of the GOV.UK Search API in `connectors/govukSearch.ts` and the reader of the NHTSA flat files in `connectors/nhtsaFlat.ts`) |
| `services/vendorResearch/sharedHosts.ts` | `isSharedHost`: the hosts where many have pages (the registry's platforms where users publish, code hosts, site builders, app hosts) and the hosts under them, which are never a key of a website match or a brand of the vendor; `isSharedPlatformHost`: exactly one of those hosts, which is no own domain of the vendor (the web session, `collectPolicyPages`) and no term of the vendor's matcher (`identity.ts`), while a user's site under one (`acme.github.io`) is both |
| `data/vrdb/` | The bundles of the lists (`<id>.json.gz`) and `manifest.json`, which each release ships |
| `utils/vrdb.utils.ts` | Queries for the copies of the lists, their loads, the answers of the read-through sources, the findings of the status job and the matches of a vendor |
| `scripts/vrdbBundle.ts` (`npm run vrdb:bundle`), `scripts/vendorResearchUnknownHosts.ts`, `scripts/vendorResearchBackfillCredibility.ts` | The build of the bundles of the lists before a release (see [Refreshing the lists for a release](#refreshing-the-lists-for-a-release)), the hosts nobody reviewed that findings cited (see [Vendor risk database](#vendor-risk-database)), and the backfill of the label (see [Backfill of the label (M3)](#backfill-of-the-label-m3)) |
| `services/vendorResearch/verifier.ts` | `verifierPasses`: the verifier, one batched model call over the findings that stay (same company? does the quote support the claim? is the source inside the lookback?); a finding with a false answer is not stored and the trace says which question it failed, and a failed call passes every finding |
| `services/vendorResearch/trace.ts` | The recorder of the [run trace](../infrastructure/web-research.md#the-run-trace) and its types |
| `services/vendorResearch/metrics.ts`, `decisionStats.ts` | The counter of the decisions on findings, and the acceptance rate by prompt version and category |
| `services/vendorResearch/eval/` | The [evaluation of the agent](../infrastructure/web-research.md#evaluating-the-agent): cases, replay, scorer, command line (`npm run eval:vendor-research`) |
| `services/vendorResearch/findings.service.ts` | `acceptFinding` and `dismissFinding` |
| `services/vendorResearch/assignee.ts` | `isUserOfOrganization`: whether a user that someone else named (the vendor's assignee, the action owner of an accepted finding) belongs to the organization; an id that cannot be a user id (zero, negative, a fraction, `NaN`, above `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER`) is answered `false` without a lookup, and a failing lookup rejects |
| `services/vendorResearch/config.ts` | The environment variables of the agent |
| `services/webResearch/` | The web research toolkit, see [Web research toolkit](../infrastructure/web-research.md): `publicUrl.ts` (URL and address policy), `guardedFetch.ts`, `robots.ts`, `pageText.ts`, `readPage.ts`, `pdfText.ts` (PDF reader), `scriptedText.ts` and `renderPage.ts` (pages that a script builds), `limiter.ts`, `webSearch.ts` (`SearchEngine`, the queue), `providerSearch.ts` (Claude's web search and the fallback), `braveSearch.ts` (Brave Search with the organization's key), `gdeltSearch.ts` (the news of the GDELT Project), `webSession.ts` (budgets, provenance, ledger), `promptText.ts`, `researchLoop.ts` |
| `services/vendors/vendorRiskCreate.service.ts` | Vendor risk creation inside the caller's transaction, for the accept of a finding (same rules as `services/vendorRisk.service.ts`) |
| `services/vendors/textSimilarity.ts` | Tokeniser and Jaccard score (risk suggestions and research de-duplication) |
| `services/inAppNotification.service.ts` | `notifyAssigneeOfVendorResearch`; the assignment email, whose context box escapes the text it shows with `escapeHtml` of `utils/sanitization.utils.ts` (the names are escaped by the mail compiler) |
| `services/vendorResearch/queue.ts` | The `vendor-research` queue and `enqueueVendorResearch`: the `vendor_research` job, its id and its options; `scheduleVendorResearchScan`: the hourly `vendor_research_scan` job |
| `services/vendorResearch/worker.ts` | `createVendorResearchWorker`: the worker of that queue (`VENDOR_RESEARCH_WORKER_CONCURRENCY` runs at a time), which carries out a run or the scan by the name of the job, started by `jobs/worker.ts` |
| `services/vendorResearch/scan.ts` | `runVendorResearchScan`: the scheduled scan (the organizations with an interval, the room under half of the daily cap, the due vendors, the scheduled starts) |
| `services/vendorResearch/sourceMemory.ts` | `keysOfSources` and `splitKnownSources`: the keys of the sources of a pack, and the sources a scheduled run sets apart |
| `services/vendorResearch/policySnapshots.ts` | The pages where the vendor states its terms: `policyKindOf`, `collectPolicyPages` (what the researcher notes as the session reads), `comparePages` (what the pipeline compares with the kept text) and the text of a change |
| `utils/vendorResearch.utils.ts` | Queries for runs and findings, the rule of which runs count toward the limits, and `getBraveKeyQuery` / `saveBraveKeyQuery` for the encrypted Brave Search key |
| `domain.layer/interfaces/i.vendorResearch.ts` | Types and the category and dismissal-reason lists; the source categories, the record databases and statuses, and the credibility labels, rules and basis |
| `database/migrations/20261010012217-create-vendor-research-tables.js` | The two tables |
| `database/migrations/20261010161236-vendor-research-notification-type.js`, `20261010161240-vendor-research-notification-unique.js` | The notification type and its dedup index |
| `database/migrations/20261011025519-vendor-research-fk-indexes.js` | Four indexes behind the foreign keys of the research tables: three on the findings, one on the runs |
| `database/migrations/20261013021400-vendor-research-web-agent.js` | Drops `search_provider` from the runs and adds `pages_read` |
| `database/migrations/20261027133032-vendor-research-run-trace.js` | Adds `trace` to the runs |
| `database/migrations/20261027133608-vendor-finding-dismiss-reasons.js` | Adds `wrong_vendor` and `outdated` to the CHECK constraint of `dismissed_reason` |
| `database/migrations/20261027153057-vendor-finding-rationale.js` | Adds `rationale` to the findings: the model's one sentence on why a finding has its likelihood and severity |
| `database/migrations/20261027155525-vendor-research-settings.js` | Creates `vendor_research_settings` |
| `database/migrations/20261027162218-vendor-research-run-progress.js` | Adds `progress` to the runs |
| `database/migrations/20261028164308-vendor-finding-snippet-only.js` | Adds `snippet_only` (boolean, `false` by default) to the findings: the mark of a finding whose quote the [check of a finding that rests on snippets](#the-check-of-a-finding-that-rests-on-snippets) did not find on the page |
| `database/migrations/20261028190447-vendor-other-names.js` | Adds `other_names` to the vendors (see [Other names](#other-names)) |
| `database/migrations/20261028214949-vendor-research-availability-category.js` | Adds the categories `availability` and `financial_health` to the CHECK constraint of the findings' `category`. Down deletes the findings of those two categories (not the vendor risks that accepted ones made) |
| `database/migrations/20261028225356-vendor-research-scheduled-scan.js` | Adds the trigger `scheduled`, `scan_interval_days` to the settings and creates `vendor_research_sources` |
| `database/migrations/20261028232304-vendor-research-snapshots.js` | Creates `vendor_research_snapshots` |
| `database/migrations/20261030223825-vendor-risk-database.js` | Creates the four `vrdb_*` tables and `vendor_vrdb_matches` |
| `database/migrations/20261030223852-vendor-finding-credibility.js` | Adds `credibility` and `credibility_basis` to the findings, lets `confidence` be null, and adds `coverage` and `registry_version` to the runs |
| `database/migrations/20261031023746-vendor-identifiers.js` | Adds `country`, `registration_number`, `lei` and `sec_cik` to the vendors, with CHECK constraints on the form of the country, the LEI and the CIK |
| `database/migrations/20261031031802-vrdb-read-through.js` | For the read-through sources: a partial index on the ids of rejected records (`vrdb_records_rejected_idx`), and `vrdb_lookups.fetched_at` may be null (a key whose first answer failed). Down removes the rows of keys that never had an answer |
| `database/migrations/20261101033134-vendor-research-search-engine.js` | Adds `search_engine` to the runs |
| `database/migrations/20261123192637-vendor-research-brave-key.js` | Adds `brave_api_key_encrypted` to the research settings |
| `database/migrations/20261123195134-vendor-research-search-engine-brave.js` | Allows `brave` in the CHECK constraint of `search_engine` |
| `database/migrations/20261111223040-rls-policies-vendor-research-tables.js` | Turns on row level security with the `tenant_isolation` policy on the six tenant tables of the research: `vendor_research_runs`, `vendor_research_settings`, `vendor_research_sources`, `vendor_research_snapshots`, `vendor_vrdb_matches` and `vendor_risk_findings`. The shared `vrdb_*` lists have no `organization_id` and no policy. Nothing changes while the app connects as the table owner (see `docs/technical/security/rls-rollout.md`) |
| `database/migrations/20261112100828-create-vendor-research-run-ledger.js` | Creates `vendor_research_run_ledger`, the ledger of the daily cap (see [Limits](#limits)), with its two indexes and the `tenant_isolation` policy, and backfills a row for every run of the last 48 hours that started. Down drops the table |

### Frontend

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `pages/Vendors/index.tsx` | Main page |
| `pages/Vendors/VendorRiskInsights/` | Content of the Insights tab: heat map, blast radius, duplicates, coverage, and `HeatCellNotice` |
| `pages/Vendors/VendorResearchFindings/` | Content of the Research findings tab: `index.tsx`, `FindingRow.tsx`, `RunSummary.tsx`, `ResearchCredits.tsx`, `DismissFindingModal.tsx`, `ResearchSettingsModal.tsx`, `InlineError.tsx`, `parseVendorId.ts`, `styles.ts` |
| `components/VendorResearchButton/` | Row button that starts research and leaves the follow hint |
| `pages/SettingsPage/SearchKey/` | The **Search API key** section of Settings → API keys: the organization's Brave Search key |
| `components/Modals/NewRisk/` | Vendor risk form; its text limits (500, 500, 1,000 characters) are the server's |
| `components/Modals/NewVendor/` | Vendor form |
| `hooks/useVendors.ts`, `hooks/useVendorRisks.tsx`, `hooks/useVendorRiskMutations.ts` | Data hooks |
| `repository/vendor.repository.ts`, `repository/vendorRisk.repository.ts` | API calls |
| `hooks/useVendorResearch.ts` | Research hooks |
| `repository/vendorResearch.repository.ts` | Research API calls and `readVendorResearchError` |
| `domain/interfaces/i.vendorResearch.ts` | Research types |
| `utils/vendorResearchText.ts`, `utils/vendorResearchAlerts.ts` | Labels and sentences for stored phrases, start messages |
| `utils/vendorResearchList.ts`, `utils/vendorResearchPlainText.ts` | The order, the groups and the summary of the findings, and the plain words of the section |
